- **archsutra-atlas**: Use this agent when the question is about business capabilities, bounded contexts, team and system ownership, governance, or build-versus-buy across several systems. Do not use it for code-level or infrastructure details. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/atlas.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-blueprint**: Use this agent when architecture documents or diagrams are needed or out of date. Typical triggers include "draw the C4 diagrams", "write the HLD and LLD", and "record this decision as an ADR". Do not use it to invent structure that is not in the code or requirements. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/blueprint.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-cloud**: Use this agent when the question is about deployment topology, networking, identity, containers or Kubernetes, infrastructure as code, disaster recovery, or regions. Typical triggers include "review our Terraform", "design the network for this workload", and "do we need multi-region". Do not use it to estimate prices without a stated source and date. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/cloud.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-data**: Use this agent when the question concerns data models, ownership, consistency, transactions, retention, or migrations. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/data.md) · ★4)

See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/forge.md) · ★4)
- **archsutra-ledger**: Use this agent when cost, budget, vendor lock-in, or operational burden drives an architecture decision. - **archsutra-neural**: Use this agent when a system uses language models, retrieval, agents, tool calling, MCP or similar protocols. - **archsutra-nexus**: Use this agent when the question concerns service-to-service or external integration: REST, GraphQL, gRPC, events, queues, webhooks, contracts, or versioning. - **archsutra-prime**: Use this agent when a user asks for a software architecture design, audit, review, modernization plan or a threat model and no single specialist is obviously right. - **archsutra-scale**: Use this agent when the concern is performance, capacity, availability, observability, or fault tolerance. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai](https://github.com/shahvatsalofficial/archsutra-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/scale.md) · ★4)

- **arm-migration**: Arm Cloud Migration Assistant accelerates moving x86 workloads to Arm infrastructure. It scans the repository for architecture assumptions, portability issues, container base image and dependency incompatibilities, and recommends Arm-optimized changes across C, C++, Go, Python, Rust, Java, and Dockerfiles. It can drive multi-arch container builds, validate performance, and guide optimization. Requires the Arm MCP server (github.com/arm/mcp) to be configured. Use PROACTIVELY when migrating a codebase or container images from x86/amd64 to ARM64/AArch64.
